# TOD HD fx -- per-pixel lighting: which MATERIAL CLASS each installed texture is.
#
#   <class>  <texture key>          (a key with a space is percent-encoded: %20)
#
# `class` names a row of tod_hd_light.txt beside the ini (roughness, metal, F0, specular, wrap, reflect).
# A texture that is in no line here takes the `default` row -- exactly what EVERY texture did before this
# file existed, which is what `light_classes = 0` still gives, byte for byte.
#
# YOU MAY EDIT THIS FILE.  A wrong class is a one-line fix: change the class word and run the installer
# again (or edit the installed copy, whose hashes the next --fx install rebuilds).  The DLL does not read
# this file -- it reads light\manifest.txt, which the INSTALLER builds from this list and the archive it
# packs, so a rebaked texture can never leave a stale hash behind.
#
# Classes the lighting path never touches (hud, sky, particle, fx_decal, shadow, envmap, caustics) are deliberately absent:
# those draws are refused by name, so a line for them would only cost work at level load.
#
# Source: work\wrapper\materials\data\classes.json (4861 textures) joined with the INSTALLED archives (hd_override.zip, 1836924070 B, mtime 1519767614) and blocks.naz
